op=="#" then return #a end elseif t==29 then local o=evalE(node[2],sc); return o[S(node[3])] elseif t==30 then local o=evalE(node[2],sc); return o[evalE(node[3],sc)] elseif t==31 or t==32 then local r={evalM(node,sc)} return r[1] elseif t==24 then local va=getva(sc); return va[1] elseif t==33 then local params=node[2]; local hasV=node[3]; local body=node[4] local csc=sc return function(...) local inner=newscope(csc) for i=1,#params do declare(inner,S(params[i]),select(i,...)) end if hasV==1 then local extra={} local total=select("#",...) for i=#params+1,total do extra[#extra+1]=select(i,...) end extra.n=#extra inner.va=extra end local r=execB(body,inner) if r and r[1]==RET then return unpack(r[2],1,r[2].n) end end elseif t==34 then local tbl={} local idx=1 local fields=node[2] local nf=#fields for i=1,nf do local f=fields[i] local ft=f[1] if ft==1 then if i==nf then local v=f[2]; local vt=v[1] if vt==31 or vt==32 or vt==24 then local r={evalM(v,sc)} for j=1,#r do tbl[idx]=r[j]; idx=idx+1 end else tbl[idx]=evalE(v,sc); idx=idx+1 end else tbl[idx]=evalE(f[2],sc); idx=idx+1 end elseif ft==2 then tbl[S(f[2])]=evalE(f[3],sc) else tbl[evalE(f[2],sc)]=evalE(f[3],sc) end end return tbl end end local function assignTo(target,val,sc) local t=target[1] if t==25 then setvar(sc,S(target[2]),val) elseif t==29 then local o=evalE(target[2],sc); o[S(target[3])]=val elseif t==30 then local o=evalE(target[2],sc); o[evalE(target[3],sc)]=val end end execS=function(node,sc) local t=node[1] if t==2 then local names=node[2]; local inits=node[3] local vals={} local ni=#inits for i=1,ni-1 do vals[#vals+1]=evalE(inits[i],sc) end if ni>0 then local last=inits[ni]; local lt=last[1] if lt==31 or lt==32 or lt==24 then local r={evalM(last,sc)}; for i=1,#r do vals[#vals+1]=r[i] end else vals[#vals+1]=evalE(last,sc) end end for i=1,#names do declare(sc,S(names[i]),vals[i]) end elseif t==3 then local targets=node[2]; local inits=node[3] local vals={} local ni=#inits for i=1,ni-1 do vals[#vals+1]=evalE(inits[i],sc) end if ni>0 then local last=inits[ni]; local lt=last[1] if lt==31 or lt==32 or lt==24 then local r={evalM(last,sc)}; for i=1,#r do vals[#vals+1]=r[i] end else vals[#vals+1]=evalE(last,sc) end end for i=1,#targets do assignTo(targets[i],vals[i],sc) end elseif t==4 then evalM(node[2],sc) elseif t==5 then local clauses=node[2] for i=1,#clauses do local c=clauses[i] if c[1]==0 then return execB(c[2],newscope(sc)) else if evalE(c[1],sc) then return execB(c[2],newscope(sc)) end end end elseif t==6 then while evalE(node[2],sc) do local r=execB(node[3],newscope(sc)) if r then if r[1]==BRK then break end if r[1]==RET then return r end end end elseif t==7 then while true do local inner=newscope(sc) local r=execB(node[3],inner) if r then if r[1]==BRK then break end if r[1]==RET then return r end end if evalE(node[2],inner) then break end end elseif t==8 then local vals={} local args=node[2]; local n=#args for i=1,n-1 do vals[#vals+1]=evalE(args[i],sc) end if n>0 then local last=args[n]; local lt=last[1] if lt==31 or lt==32 or lt==24 then local r={evalM(last,sc)}; for i=1,#r do vals[#vals+1]=r[i] end else vals[#vals+1]=evalE(last,sc) end end vals.n=#vals return {RET,vals} elseif t==9 then return {BRK} elseif t==10 then return execB(node[2],newscope(sc)) elseif t==11 then local a=evalE(node[3],sc); local b=evalE(node[4],sc) local step=node[5]; local c if type(step)=="table" then c=evalE(step,sc) else c=1 end local i=a local nm=S(node[2]) while (c>0 and i<=b) or (c<0 and i>=b) do local inner=newscope(sc) declare(inner,nm,i) local r=execB(node[6],inner) if r then if r[1]==BRK then break end if r[1]==RET then return r end end i=i+c end elseif t==12 then local names=node[2]; local iters=node[3]; local body=node[4] local vals={} local ni=#iters for i=1,ni-1 do vals[#vals+1]=evalE(iters[i],sc) end if ni>0 then local last=iters[ni]; local lt=last[1] if lt==31 or lt==32 or lt==24 then local r={evalM(last,sc)}; for i=1,#r do vals[#vals+1]=r[i] end else vals[#vals+1]=evalE(last,sc) end end local f,s,var=vals[1],vals[2],vals[3] while true do local r={f(s,var)} var=r[1] if var==nil then break end local inner=newscope(sc) for i=1,#names do declare(inner,S(names[i]),r[i]) end local br=execB(body,inner) if br then if br[1]==BRK then break end if br[1]==RET then return br end end end elseif t==13 then local isLocal=node[2]; local ident=node[3]; local func=node[4] if isLocal==1 and ident[1]==25 then local nm=S(ident[2]) declare(sc,nm,nil) local fn=evalE(func,sc) local b=lookup(sc,nm); b[1]=fn else assignTo(ident,evalE(func,sc),sc) end end end execB=function(block,sc) for i=1,#block do local r=execS(block[i],sc) if r then return r end end end local root=newscope(nil,{n=0}) local r=execB(_01I00O0l0l220[2],root) if r and r[1]==RET then return unpack(r[2],1,r[2].n) end
